### /Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/worker/api/catalog.ts

```ts
import { Hono } from "hono";
import type { Env, R2SqlResponse } from "../types";
import { R2_SQL_ACCOUNT_ID, R2_SQL_BUCKET_NAME } from "../types";

const catalog = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();

async function executeR2Sql(token: string, sql: string): Promise<R2SqlResponse> {
  const r2SqlUrl = `https://api.sql.cloudflarestorage.com/api/v1/accounts/${R2_SQL_ACCOUNT_ID}/r2-sql/query/${R2_SQL_BUCKET_NAME}`;
  const response = await fetch(r2SqlUrl, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ query: sql }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`R2 SQL error: ${response.status}`);
  return (await response.json()) as R2SqlResponse;
}

function quoteIdentifier(id: string): string {
  return `"${id.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
}

// GET /api/catalog/tables
catalog.get("/tables", async (c) => {
  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();
  if (!token) return c.json({ error: "R2 SQL token not configured" }, 500);

  const namespaces = await executeR2Sql(token, "SHOW NAMESPACES");
  const tables = (
    await Promise.all(
      (namespaces.result?.rows ?? []).map(async (ns) => {
        const nsName = Object.values(ns)[0] as string;
        const tblResult = await executeR2Sql(token, `SHOW TABLES IN ${quoteIdentifier(nsName)}`);
        return (tblResult.result?.rows ?? []).map((tbl) => ({
          namespace: nsName,
          table: Object.values(tbl)[0] as string,
        }));
      })
    )
  ).flat();

  return c.json({ tables });
});

// GET /api/catalog/describe/:namespace/:table
catalog.get("/describe/:namespace/:table", async (c) => {
  const { namespace, table } = c.req.param();
  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();
  if (!token) return c.json({ error: "R2 SQL token not configured" }, 500);

  const raw = await executeR2Sql(
    token,
    `DESCRIBE ${quoteIdentifier(namespace)}.${quoteIdentifier(table)}`
  );
  return c.json({ data: raw.result?.rows ?? [] });
});

export default catalog;
```

    "@phosphor-icons/react": "^2.1.0",

    "react": "^19.2.4",
    "react-dom": "^19.2.4"
  },
  "devDependencies": {
    "@biomejs/biome": "^2.4.11",
    "@cloudflare/vite-plugin": "^1.0.0",
    "@cloudflare/workers-types": "^4.20250124.0",
    "@types/react": "^19.2.14",
    "@types/react-dom": "^19.2.3",
    "@vitejs/plugin-react": "^6.0.1",
    "typescript": "~6.0.2",
    "vite": "^8.0.4",
    "wrangler": "^4.4.0"
  }
}
```

---

## Architecture Summary

### Stack

- **Frontend**: React 19, Vite 8, TypeScript 6
- **Editor**: CodeMirror 6 (imperative, not wrapped in a React-specific library)
- **Worker/Backend**: Cloudflare Worker via Hono 4, deployed with Wrangler 4
- **Linter**: Biome (not ESLint/Prettier)

### Installed CodeMirror Packages

Only three packages are installed — no autocomplete, language server, or extra extension packages beyond the bundled `basicSetup`:

| Package | Version | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `codemirror` | ^6.0.2 | Umbrella package; provides `basicSetup` |
| `@codemirror/lang-sql` | ^6.10.0 | SQL syntax highlighting and parsing |
| `@codemirror/state` | ^6.6.0 | `EditorState` |
| `@codemirror/view` | ^6.41.0 | `EditorView`, `keymap` |

No `@codemirror/autocomplete`, `@codemirror/language`, `@codemirror/lint`, or similar packages are installed.

### Editor.tsx — CodeMirror Setup

The editor mounts imperatively in a `useEffect([], [])` (runs once). Extensions applied:

1. `basicSetup` — standard line numbers, bracket matching, search, history, etc.
2. `sql()` — SQL syntax highlighting (no schema/autocomplete config passed)
3. `keymap.of([{ key: "Mod-Enter", run: ... }])` — Cmd/Ctrl+Enter triggers `onRun`
4. `EditorView.updateListener` — fires `onChange` on every doc change
5. `EditorView.theme(...)` — inline theme: 100% height, scrollable

A second `useEffect([value])` syncs external value changes by dispatching a full-document replace. Callback refs (`onChangeRef`, `onRunRef`) prevent stale closure issues without requiring the mount effect to re-run.

The `sql()` extension is called with no arguments — there is no `schema` option passed, so no table/column autocomplete is wired up yet.

### useCatalog Hook — What It Returns

```ts
{ tables: TableInfo[], loadingTables: boolean, loadColumns: (namespace, table) => Promise<void> }
```

- On mount, calls `GET /api/catalog/tables` and populates `tables` with `{ namespace, table }` objects (no columns yet).
- `loadColumns` is a stable `useCallback` that fetches `GET /api/catalog/describe/:namespace/:table` and merges column data into the matching entry in `tables`.
- The `columns` field on `TableInfo` is typed as `DescribeResult["data"]` = `Record<string, unknown>[] | undefined`.

### App.tsx — Component Wiring

App holds two pieces of state: `sql` (string) and delegates to two hooks:

- `useQuery()` — provides `{ result, loading, execute }` (not read in this pass but used for run/result flow)
- `useCatalog()` — provides `{ tables, loadingTables }` (note: `loadColumns` is destructured but not used directly in App — it is consumed by `Sidebar`)

Data flow:
```
App
 ├── Sidebar(tables, loading, onInsert)   ← catalog tree; clicking a table calls onInsert
 ├── Editor(value=sql, onChange=setSql, onRun=handleRun)
 ├── Toolbar(loading, onRun, result)
 └── ResultPanel(result, loading)
```

`handleInsert` appends text to the SQL string, which triggers the second `useEffect` in `Editor` to sync the view.

### Types

```ts
interface TableInfo {          // defined in useCatalog.ts
  namespace: string;
  table: string;
  columns?: Record<string, unknown>[];
}

interface QueryResult {        // src/lib/api.ts
  data?: Record<string, unknown>[];
  columns?: string[];
  error?: string;
  elapsed?: number;
  engine?: string;
  status?: number;
}

interface TablesResult {       // src/lib/api.ts
  tables: { namespace: string; table: string }[];
  error?: string;
}

interface DescribeResult {     // src/lib/api.ts
  data?: Record<string, unknown>[];
  error?: string;
}
```

### Worker — /describe Endpoint

`GET /api/catalog/describe/:namespace/:table` returns:

```json
{ "data": [ ...rows from DESCRIBE "namespace"."table" ] }
```

Each row is a raw `Record<string, unknown>` — the column schema format is whatever Cloudflare R2 SQL returns for a `DESCRIBE` statement (field names like `name`, `type`, `nullable` are expected but not typed on the worker side).

The worker uses `SHOW NAMESPACES` then `SHOW TABLES IN "ns"` in parallel to build the table list. Token is retrieved from a Cloudflare KV binding (`R2_SQL_TOKEN`).

### Key Gap for Autocomplete

The `sql()` extension receives no `schema` argument. To add autocomplete you would need to:

1. Build a `SQLNamespace` / `schema` object from the `tables` and `columns` data already available in `useCatalog`
2. Pass it as `sql({ schema: ... })` or use `SQLConfig` with `dialect` + `schema`
3. Either re-create the EditorView when the schema changes, or use `view.dispatch` with a `StateEffect` to reconfigure the schema extension dynamically
```

[];\n        setTables(list.map((t) => ({ ...t })));\n\n        const described = await Promise.all(\n          list.map(async (t) => {\n            const desc = await describeTable(t.namespace, t.table);\n            return {\n              fqn: `${t.namespace}.${t.table}`,\n              columns: desc.data ?? [],\n            };\n          })\n        );\n\n        const newSchema: SqlSchema = {};\n        const enriched: TableInfo[] = [];\n        for (const d of described) {\n          newSchema[d.fqn] = d.columns.map((c) => c.column_name);\n          const [ns, tbl] = d.fqn.split(\".\");\n          enriched.push({ namespace: ns, table: tbl, columns: d.columns });\n        }\n        setTables(enriched);\n        setSchema(newSchema);\n      })\n      .catch(() => setTables([]))\n      .finally(() => setLoadingTables(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  return { tables, schema, loadingTables };\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: biome check + tsc**\n\nRun: `cd sql-editor && pnpm exec biome check --write . && pnpm exec tsc -b`\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add sql-editor/src/lib/api.ts `sql-editor/src/components/Editor.tsx`\n- Modify: `sql-editor/src/App.tsx`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: `Editor.tsx` に `schema` prop と `Compartment` を追加**\n\n```typescript\n// sql-editor/src/components/Editor.tsx — 全体を以下に置き換え\nimport { sql } from \"@codemirror/lang-sql\";\nimport { Compartment, EditorState } from \"@codemirror/state\";\nimport { EditorView, keymap } from \"@codemirror/view\";\nimport { basicSetup } from \"codemirror\";\nimport { useEffect, useRef } from \"react\";\nimport type { SqlSchema } from \"../hooks/useCatalog\";\n\ninterface EditorProps {\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (value: string) => void;\n  onRun: () => void;\n  schema?: SqlSchema;\n}\n\nexport default function Editor({ value, onChange, onRun, schema }: EditorProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const viewRef = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);\n  const sqlCompartment = useRef(new Compartment());\n  const onChangeRef = useRef(onChange);\n  const onRunRef = useRef(onRun);\n\n  onChangeRef.current = onChange;\n  onRunRef.current = onRun;\n\n  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: CodeMirror is imperative; value is only used for initial doc, subsequent syncs happen via the second useEffect\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!containerRef.current) return;\n\n    const view = new EditorView({\n      state: EditorState.create({\n        doc: value,\n        extensions: [\n          basicSetup,\n          sqlCompartment.current.of(sql()),\n          keymap.of([\n            {\n              key: \"Mod-Enter\",\n              run: () => {\n                onRunRef.current();\n                return true;\n              },\n            },\n          ]),\n          EditorView.updateListener.of((update) => {\n            if (update.docChanged) {\n              onChangeRef.current(update.state.doc.toString());\n            }\n          }),\n          EditorView.theme({\n            \"&\": { height: \"100%\" },\n            \".cm-scroller\": { overflow: \"auto\" },\n            \".cm-content\": { padding: \"12px 0\" },\n          }),\n        ],\n      }),\n      parent: containerRef.current,\n    });\n\n    viewRef.current = view;\n    return () => view.destroy();\n  }, []);\n\n  // Sync external value changes (e.g., sidebar insert)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const view = viewRef.current;\n    if (view && view.state.doc.toString() !== value) {\n      view.dispatch({\n        changes: { from: 0, to: view.state.doc.length, insert: value },\n      });\n    }\n  }, [value]);\n\n  // Update SQL schema when catalog data arrives\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const view = viewRef.current;\n    if (view && schema && Object.keys(schema).length > 0) {\n      view.dispatch({\n        effects: sqlCompartment.current.reconfigure(sql({ schema })),\n      });\n    }\n  }, [schema]);\n\n  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ height: \"100%\", overflow: \"auto\" }} />;\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: `App.tsx` で `schema` を `Editor` に渡す**\n\n`useCatalog` から `schema` を取得し、`Editor` コンポーネントに渡す。\n\n```tsx\n// sql-editor && pnpm exec playwright test --reporter=list`\nExpected: 全テストがパス（dev server は playwright.config.ts の webServer で自動起動）\n\n注意: テストは実際の R2 SQL API に対して実行される。ローカル `.dev.vars` に `R2_SQL_TOKEN` が設定されている必要がある。\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: biome check**\n\nRun: `cd sql-editor && pnpm exec biome check --write .`\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: コミット**\n\n```bash\ngit add sql-editor/e2e/\ngit commit -m \"test: add 5	```
6	Task tool (general-purpose):
7	  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
8	  prompt: |
9	    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
10	
11	    ## Task Description
12	
13	    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
14	
15	    ## Context
16	
17	    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
18	
19	    ## Before You Begin
20	
21	    If you have questions about:
22	    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
23	    - The approach or implementation strategy
24	    - Dependencies or assumptions
25	    - Anything unclear in the task description
26	
27	    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
28	
29	    ## Your Job
30	
31	    Once you're clear on requirements:
32	    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
33	    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
34	    3. Verify implementation works
35	    4. Commit your work
36	    5. Self-review (see below)
37	    6. Report back
38	
39	    Work from: [directory]
40	
41	    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
42	    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
43	
44	    ## Code Organization
45	
46	    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
47	    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
48	    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
49	    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
50	    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
51	      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
52	    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
53	      and note it as a concern in your report
54	    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
55	      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
56	
57	    ## When You're in Over Your Head
58	
59	    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
60	    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
61	
62	    **STOP and escalate when:**
63	    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
64	    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
65	    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
66	    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
67	    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
68	
69	    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
70	    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
71	    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
72	    or break the task into smaller pieces.
73	
74	    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
75	
76	    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
77	
78	    **Completeness:**
79	    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
80	    - Did I miss any requirements?
81	    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
82	
83	    **Quality:**
84	    - Is this my best work?
85	    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
86	    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
87	
88	    **Discipline:**
89	    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
90	    - Did I only build what was requested?
91	    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
92	
93	    **Testing:**
94	    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
95	    - Did I follow TDD if required?
96	    - Are tests comprehensive?
97	
98	    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
99	
100	    ## Report Format
101	
102	    When done, report:
103	    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
104	    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
105	    - What you tested and test results
106	    - Files changed
107	    - Self-review findings (if any)
108	    - Any issues or concerns
109	
110	    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
111	    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
112	    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.
113	```

{
  "description": "Implement Task 1: useCatalog schema",
  "model": "sonnet",
  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 1 of the SQL Editor Phase 1.5 plan: \"useCatalog でスキーマ自動取得\"\n\n## Task Description\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts`\n- Modify: `sql-editor/src/lib/api.ts` (DescribeResult の型を具体化)\n\n### Step 1: `api.ts` の `DescribeResult` 型を具体化\n\n`Record<string, unknown>[]` では型が曖昧なので、実際の DESCRIBE レスポンスに合わせる。実際のレスポンスは `{ column_name, type, required, initial_default, write_default, doc }` の形式（confirmed via curl on production endpoint).\n\n```typescript\n// sql-editor/src/lib/api.ts — DescribeResult を以下に変更\nexport interface ColumnInfo {\n  column_name: string;\n  type: string;\n  required: string;\n}\n\nexport interface DescribeResult {\n  data?: ColumnInfo[];\n  error?: string;\n}\n```\n\n### Step 2: `useCatalog.ts` でスキーマオブジェクトを自動構築\n\nテーブル一覧取得後に、全テーブルの DESCRIBE を並列実行し、CodeMirror 用の `schema` オブジェクトを構築する。\n\n```typescript\n// sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts — 全体を以下に置き換え\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { type ColumnInfo, type TablesResult, describeTable, fetchTables } from \"../lib/api\";\n\nexport interface TableInfo {\n  namespace: string;\n  table: string;\n  columns?: ColumnInfo[];\n}\n\nexport type SqlSchema = Record<string, string[]>;\n\nexport function useCatalog() {\n  const [tables, setTables] = useState<TableInfo[]>([]);\n  const [schema, setSchema] = useState<SqlSchema>({});\n  const [loadingTables, setLoadingTables] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchTables()\n      .then(async (res: TablesResult) => {\n        const list = res.tables ?? [];\n        setTables(list.map((t) => ({ ...t })));\n\n        const described = await Promise.all(\n          list.map(async (t) => {\n            const desc = await describeTable(t.namespace, t.table);\n            return {\n              fqn: `${t.namespace}.${t.table}`,\n              columns: desc.data ?? [],\n            };\n          })\n        );\n\n        const newSchema: SqlSchema = {};\n        const enriched: TableInfo[] = [];\n        for (const d of described) {\n          newSchema[d.fqn] = d.columns.map((c) => c.column_name);\n          const [ns, tbl] = d.fqn.split(\".\");\n          enriched.push({ namespace: ns, table: tbl, columns: d.columns });\n        }\n        setTables(enriched);\n        setSchema(newSchema);\n      })\n      .catch(() => setTables([]))\n      .finally(() => setLoadingTables(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  return { tables, schema, loadingTables };\n}\n```\n\n### Step 3: biome check + tsc\n\nRun: `cd sql-editor && pnpm exec biome check --write . && pnpm exec tsc -b`\n\n### Step 4: コミット\n\n```bash\ngit add sql-editor/src/lib/api.ts sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat: auto-fetch column schema for all tables in useCatalog\"\n```\n\n## Context\n\nThe SQL Editor at `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/` is a Vite + React + Cloudflare Workers project that lets users execute SQL queries against R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg tables). This is Phase 1.5 of the project — Phase 1 (basic editor + R2 SQL proxy) is already complete and deployed.\n\nCurrently:\n- `useCatalog` hook only fetches table list (namespace + table name) on mount, not columns\n- `DescribeResult.data` is typed as `Record<string, unknown>[]`\n- The hook also has a `loadColumns` function that's not actually used anywhere — Task 1 removes it because we'll auto-fetch all columns instead\n- `Sidebar.tsx` uses `TableInfo` from `useCatalog` — make sure your changes don't break its expectations (it only reads `namespace` and `table` fields, so adding `columns?` is backward-compatible, but removing fields isn't allowed)\n\nThe next task (Task 2) will use the new `schema` field from `useCatalog` to wire CodeMirror's schema-aware autocomplete via `Compartment`. That's why we're building the schema as `Record<string, string[]>` (CodeMirror's `sql({ schema })` expected format).\n\n**Important behavioral details:**\n- The previous `useCatalog` exposed `loadColumns` — we are intentionally removing it since columns are now auto-fetched on mount. Verify no other file imports it.\n- Both `tables` and `schema` should be populated together (after the Promise.all completes). Avoid showing partial state.\n- If `fetchTables` succeeds but a `describeTable` call fails, the whole `.then` will reject — that's acceptable for MVP.\n\n## Working directory\n\n`/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare`\n\n## Before You Begin\n\nIf you have questions about:\n- The requirements or acceptance criteria\n- Whether removing loadColumns is safe (check Sidebar.tsx and any other consumer)\n- Anything unclear\n\n**Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.\n\n## Your Job\n\nOnce you're clear on requirements:\n1. Implement exactly what the task specifies\n2. Verify biome check and tsc pass\n3. Commit your work\n4. Self-review (see below)\n5. Report back\n\n## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review\n\nReview your work with fresh eyes:\n- Did I fully implement everything in the spec?\n- Did I check that no other file imports `loadColumns`?\n- Does `Sidebar.tsx` still work with the new `TableInfo` type?\n- Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?\n\n## Report Format\n\nWhen done, report:\n- **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT\n- What you implemented\n- What you tested and test results\n- Files changed\n- Self-review findings\n- Any issues or concerns"
}

---

**Status:** DONE

**What was implemented:**

- `api.ts`: Added `ColumnInfo` interface (`{ column_name, type, required }`) and narrowed `DescribeResult.data` from `Record<string, unknown>[]` to `ColumnInfo[]`.
- `useCatalog.ts`: Full replacement per spec — removed `useCallback`/`loadColumns`, added `schema: SqlSchema` state, and on mount: fetch table list, then `Promise.all` all `describeTable` calls in parallel, then atomically set both `tables` (enriched with `columns`) and `schema` (`{ "ns.table": ["col1", "col2", ...] }`).

**Tests / verification:**

- `pnpm exec biome check --write .` — passed (fixed one import ordering in `useCatalog.ts` automatically).
- `pnpm exec tsc -b` — passed with no errors.

**Files changed:**

- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/src/lib/api.ts`
- `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts`

**Self-review findings:**

- `loadColumns` only existed in `useCatalog.ts` — confirmed by grep, no other file imported it. Safe to remove.
- `App.tsx` already destructured only `{ tables, loadingTables }` — adding `schema` to the return is additive, no breakage.
- `Sidebar.tsx` only reads `namespace` and `table` from `TableInfo` — adding `columns?` is backward-compatible.
- No overbuilding: exactly what the spec asked for, nothing more.

Now I have a complete picture. Here is the review.

---

## Code Review: Task 1 — useCatalog auto-fetch column schema

### Strengths

- **Responsibility is clear and well-bounded.** `api.ts` owns HTTP concerns only. `useCatalog.ts` owns React state and orchestration. The boundary is clean and unchanged from before.
- **`ColumnInfo` type is correct.** `required: string` is right given the actual API returns `"true"`/`"false"` as strings. The narrowing from `Record<string, unknown>[]` is a meaningful improvement.
- **`SqlSchema` type is appropriately placed.** Exporting it from `useCatalog.ts` rather than `api.ts` is correct — it is a UI-layer concern (CodeMirror shape), not an API concern.
- **File sizes stayed minimal.** `useCatalog.ts` grew from ~33 to ~49 lines. No bloat.
- **`Promise.all` parallelism** is the right approach for N independent describe calls.
- **Removing `loadColumns`** simplifies the consumer contract. The hook's surface area got smaller while its capability got larger, which is good design.
- **No stale closure issues.** The `useEffect` captures no mutable state from the render scope — it is entirely self-contained.

---

### Issues

#### Critical

None.

#### Important

**1. Namespace splitting is fragile — silently corrupts data when namespaces contain dots.**

In `useCatalog.ts` line 37:
```typescript
const [ns, tbl] = d.fqn.split(".");
```
If `namespace` itself contains a dot (e.g. `streaming.v2`), then `fqn` becomes `streaming.v2.linear_events`, and `split(".")` yields `["streaming", "v2", "linear_events"]`. The destructuring assigns `ns = "streaming"`, `tbl = "v2"`, silently dropping the actual table name. The original `t.namespace` and `t.table` are already available in the outer `list` array — there is no reason to round-trip through the FQN string. The simplest fix is to carry `namespace` and `table` forward directly:

```typescript
const described = await Promise.all(
  list.map(async (t) => {
    const desc = await describeTable(t.namespace, t.table);
    return { namespace: t.namespace, table: t.table, columns: desc.data ?? [] };
  })
);

const newSchema: SqlSchema = {};
const enriched: TableInfo[] = [];
for (const d of described) {
  const fqn = `${d.namespace}.${d.table}`;
  newSchema[fqn] = d.columns.map((c) => c.column_name);
  enriched.push({ namespace: d.namespace, table: d.table, columns: d.columns });
}
```

This eliminates the string-parse entirely.

**2. Intermediate `setTables` call on line 21 is a ghost state flush with no consumer value.**

```typescript
setTables(list.map((t) => ({ ...t })));  // columns = undefined
// ... await Promise.all(...)
setTables(enriched);                      // columns populated
```

The first `setTables` causes a render where `tables` has no `columns`, then immediately a second render replaces it. No current consumer uses the column-less intermediate state — `Sidebar` only uses `namespace` and `table`, and `App.tsx` does not destructure `schema` yet. This costs a wasted re-render and adds mental overhead. For MVP it is harmless, but the intermediate render is not intentional — it is an artifact of the sequential await structure. Removing `setTables(list.map(...))` and only calling `setTables(enriched)` after the `Promise.all` resolves is cleaner and correct.

#### Minor

**3. `describeTable` errors are silently swallowed per-table.**

If any single `describeTable` call fails, `Promise.all` rejects and the outer `.catch` sets `tables` to `[]`, discarding even the table list that was already fetched successfully. For MVP this is acceptable, but the failure mode is surprising: a single bad `DESCRIBE` on one table wipes out the entire sidebar. A more resilient approach is `Promise.allSettled` with per-entry fallback to empty columns. Worth noting for a future iteration.

**4. `schema` state is not reset on the `.catch` path.**

When `.catch(() => setTables([]))` fires, `schema` remains `{}` (its initial value), which is correct by accident. This is fine, but if `useCatalog` ever gains a reload/refresh capability, both `tables` and `schema` would need to be reset together. Not a problem now.

**5. `App.tsx` does not yet consume `schema`.**

`schema` is returned from `useCatalog()` but `App.tsx` destructures only `{ tables, loadingTables }`. This is expected since CodeMirror wiring is Task 2. No action needed — just confirming the plumbing is ready.

---

### Final Assessment

**Approved with minor issues.**

Issue #1 (dot-in-namespace FQN split) is the one substantive correctness risk. It will not affect the current data (`streaming.linear_events` has a single-segment namespace), but it is a latent bug that will silently corrupt data if namespaces ever use dots. Fix it by preserving `t.namespace`/`t.table` directly rather than parsing the FQN string. Issue #2 (wasted intermediate render) is a one-line cleanup worth doing alongside that fix.

Both issues in `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts` have been fixed:

**Issue 1 (FQN split fragility):** Removed the intermediate `fqn` string from the Promise.all return value. Each entry now carries `namespace` and `table` directly, and the schema key is constructed as `` `${d.namespace}.${d.table}` `` only for the schema record — never parsed back. This is safe even if a namespace contains dots.

**Issue 2 (Wasted intermediate render):** Removed the `setTables(list.map((t) => ({ ...t })))` call that happened before `await Promise.all(...)`. State is now set exactly once after all column descriptions have resolved.

`biome check --write` and `tsc -b` both passed cleanly. Committed as `9109a41` on branch `feat/sql-editor-phase1.5`.

  "prompt": "You are reviewing whether an implementation matches its specification.\n\n## What Was Requested\n\nTask 2 of SQL Editor Phase 1.5: Add schema-aware autocomplete via Compartment.\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: sql-editor/src/components/Editor.tsx\n- Modify: sql-editor/src/App.tsx\n\n### Editor.tsx must contain (full replacement):\n\n```typescript\nimport { sql } from \"@codemirror/lang-sql\";\nimport { Compartment, EditorState } from \"@codemirror/state\";\nimport { EditorView, keymap } from \"@codemirror/view\";\nimport { basicSetup } from \"codemirror\";\nimport { useEffect, useRef } from \"react\";\nimport type { SqlSchema } from \"../hooks/useCatalog\";\n\ninterface EditorProps {\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (value: string) => void;\n  onRun: () => void;\n  schema?: SqlSchema;\n}\n\nexport default function Editor({ value, onChange, onRun, schema }: EditorProps) {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const viewRef = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);\n  const sqlCompartment = useRef(new Compartment());\n  const onChangeRef = useRef(onChange);\n  const onRunRef = useRef(onRun);\n\n  onChangeRef.current = onChange;\n  onRunRef.current = onRun;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!containerRef.current) return;\n    const view = new EditorView({\n      state: EditorState.create({\n        doc: value,\n        extensions: [\n          basicSetup,\n          sqlCompartment.current.of(sql()),\n          keymap.of([{ key: \"Mod-Enter\", run: () => { onRunRef.current(); return true; } }]),\n          EditorView.updateListener.of((update) => { if (update.docChanged) { onChangeRef.current(update.state.doc.toString()); } }),\n          EditorView.theme({ \"&\": { height: \"100%\" }, \".cm-scroller\": { overflow: \"auto\" }, \".cm-content\": { padding: \"12px 0\" } }),\n        ],\n      }),\n      parent: containerRef.current,\n    });\n    viewRef.current = view;\n    return () => view.destroy();\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const view = viewRef.current;\n    if (view && view.state.doc.toString() !== value) {\n      view.dispatch({ changes: { from: 0, to: view.state.doc.length, insert: value } });\n    }\n  }, [value]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const view = viewRef.current;\n    if (view && schema && Object.keys(schema).length > 0) {\n      view.dispatch({ effects: sqlCompartment.current.reconfigure(sql({ schema })) });\n    }\n  }, [schema]);\n\n  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ height: \"100%\", overflow: \"auto\" }} />;\n}\n```\n\n### App.tsx must:\n1. id: toolu_0197sXy2wwJsyvPoxdEtGZHv
```
Here is the complete review.

---

## Code Review: Task 2 — Schema-Aware SQL Autocomplete via Compartment

### Strengths

- **Compartment is stable across renders.** `useRef(new Compartment())` is correct. `useRef` initialises once; the `new Compartment()` call happens only during the first render, so the identity is preserved across re-renders. This is the canonical pattern for mutable, non-React objects that must survive the component lifecycle.
- **Three useEffects are cleanly separated.** Mount (EditorView creation + cleanup), value sync, and schema sync are each in their own effect with a precise dependency array. There is no cross-concern bleed.
- **Stale-closure protection is working.** `onChangeRef` and `onRunRef` are updated on every render (lines 22–23) and are referenced inside the mount effect closure. The schema effect depends on `[schema]`, so it re-runs whenever the reference changes, and it reads `sqlCompartment.current` directly — not a closed-over stale value.
- **Memory safety.** `view.destroy()` is called in the mount effect's cleanup. The schema effect holds only a local `view` variable; there is no lingering reference after the function returns.
- **TypeScript is clean.** `schema?: SqlSchema`, the `SqlSchema = Record<string, string[]>` alias, and the `import type` for the interface are all correct.
- **Single responsibility holds.** Editor.tsx remains a controlled CodeMirror host. It receives schema as a prop and delegates all data-fetching to the caller. App.tsx acts as a thin wiring layer only.
- **Removed eslint-disable comment.** The `// eslint-disable-next-line` comment on the empty dependency array is gone. The biome-ignore comment that replaced it is precise and describes the actual reasoning.

---

### Issues

#### Critical

None.

---

#### Important

**`Object.keys(schema).length > 0` guard cannot handle schema being explicitly cleared**

At line 76:
```typescript
if (view && schema && Object.keys(schema).length > 0) {
```

The `schema && ...` check is redundant (`SqlSchema` is `Record<string, string[]>`, never `null`/`undefined` at runtime because `useCatalog` initialises it to `{}` and the prop is typed `schema?: SqlSchema` — when undefined the outer `schema &&` short-circuits, which is fine). The real concern is the `Object.keys(schema).length > 0` guard: if `schema` is intentionally reset to `{}` (e.g., on error or logout), the Compartment is never reconfigured back to `sql()` with no schema. The autocomplete would silently remain stale with the previous schema.

Currently `useCatalog` only ever grows the schema (no reset path), so this is not a live bug, but it is a fragile contract. If the hook ever adds a reset/refresh path, Editor.tsx will behave incorrectly without any indication.

Two options:
1. Drop the `Object.keys` guard entirely and always dispatch on any `schema` change (including `{}`). An empty schema passed to `sql({ schema: {} })` is harmless.
2. Keep the guard but document explicitly why the reset case is intentionally unhandled.

Recommended fix:

```typescript
useEffect(() => {
  const view = viewRef.current;
  if (!view || schema === undefined) return;
  view.dispatch({
    effects: sqlCompartment.current.reconfigure(sql({ schema })),
  });
}, [schema]);
```

This dispatches on every defined schema value (including `{}`), handles the reset case, and removes the misleading key-count guard.

---

#### Minor

**`schema` initialises as `{}` in useCatalog, causing a no-op dispatch on first render**

`useCatalog` returns `schema` initialised to `{}`. On the first render, `Editor` receives `schema={}`. The effect runs but `Object.keys({}).length > 0` is false, so nothing happens — this is the correct result. However, if the `Object.keys` guard were removed per the recommendation above, a dispatch would fire on mount with an empty schema. That is still correct (configuring `sql({ schema: {} })` is equivalent to `sql()`), but it is an extra, avoidable round-trip through CodeMirror's state machine.

To avoid this, the guard could be changed from a key-count check to an explicit previous-value comparison, or `useCatalog` could initialise `schema` to `undefined` instead of `{}` and use `schema?: SqlSchema` throughout:

```typescript
const [schema, setSchema] = useState<SqlSchema | undefined>(undefined);
```

This would let the Editor effect depend on `schema` being `undefined` (not yet loaded) vs. a populated record, with no spurious dispatch.

This is a minor efficiency concern, not a correctness issue.

---

**`schema` prop being optional causes a silent no-autocomplete-ever scenario**

`schema` is typed `schema?: SqlSchema`. If a caller omits it, the schema effect never fires and the editor silently runs without autocomplete. There is no warning, no fallback, and no indication to future developers that omitting this prop degrades UX. This is acceptable for an optional enhancement, but worth noting if this component is ever reused beyond App.tsx.

---

### Final Assessment

**Approved with minor issues**

The implementation is architecturally correct. `useRef(new Compartment())` is the right pattern. The three effects are well-isolated. Stale-closure protection is sound. TypeScript is strict-compliant. There are no memory leaks or React rules-of-hooks violations.

The one "Important" issue — the `Object.keys` guard silently ignoring schema resets — is not a live bug given the current useCatalog implementation, but it creates a fragile assumption between the hook and the component. Removing or narrowing the guard before any reset/refresh path is added to useCatalog would prevent a future silent regression.

**Status: BLOCKED**

## What was implemented

Created `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts` with all 8 required E2E tests. Biome check passed. The file is committed to `feat/sql-editor-phase1.5`.

## Test execution results: 1 passed, 7 failed

The single passing test ("エディターが表示され SQL を入力できる") confirms the dev server starts correctly and the CodeMirror editor renders.

All 7 remaining tests fail with the same root cause:
```
Error: Secret "R2_SQL_TOKEN" not found
```

## Root cause

`R2_SQL_TOKEN` is a **Cloudflare Secrets Store** binding — not a plain secret. Unlike regular secrets (which go in `.dev.vars`), Secrets Store bindings require a separate local provisioning step via wrangler:

```bash
cd sql-editor
pnpm exec wrangler secrets-store secret create 16dbae494da74139a51c1b15d7a7e6d1 \
  --name R2_SQL_TOKEN \
  --value <YOUR_TOKEN_VALUE> \
  --scopes workers
```

This stores the secret in `.wrangler/state/v3/` (local miniflare persistence), which `@cloudflare/vite-plugin` reads at dev time. The store ID is `16dbae494da74139a51c1b15d7a7e6d1` (from `wrangler.jsonc`).

**To unblock:** Run the command above with the actual R2 SQL token value. Once the local secret is seeded, re-run `pnpm exec playwright test --reporter=list` and all 8 tests should pass.

## Files changed

- Created: `sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts` (8 tests, committed)

| | 説明 | メリット | デメリット |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A** | `.dev.vars` を作成（R2 トークンをローカルに） | テストが速い、ローカル開発も動く | トークンを別途用意する必要 |
| **B** | プロダクション URL でテスト | セットアップ不要、本物の環境を検証 | 遅い、本番依存 |
| **C** | API 不要なテストだけ残す（編集器表示など） | 外部依存なし | 価値が低い |

> AGENT

1. **R2 SQL Token を取得**
   - Cloudflare Dashboard → R2 → `lake` バケット → Settings → **R2 API Tokens** → 既存のトークン、または新規作成
   - または、過去に登録済みのトークン値が手元にあればそれを使用

2. **`.dev.vars` を作成**

   ```bash
   echo 'R2_SQL_TOKEN=[REDACTED]' > sql-editor/.dev.vars
   ```

3. **テスト実行で動作確認**

   ```bash
   cd sql-editor && pnpm exec playwright test --reporter=list
   ```

`.dev.vars` は既に `.gitignore` に入っているので、誤ってコミットされる心配はありません。

セットアップが完了したら教えてください。E2E テストを実行して Task 4 を進めます。

> AGENT

4 テストが通過、4 つが失敗。失敗している全てが `Meta+Enter`（Cmd+Enter）を使うテストです。Run ボタンを使うテストは通っています。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Playwright の headless chromium で `Meta+Enter` が CodeMirror の `Mod-Enter` キーマップに伝わらない既知の挙動パターン
- `click → type` は動作するが、修飾キー付きの Enter キー伝搬だけ失敗するケースが多い
- 解決策: Cmd+Enter テストだけを残し、他のテストは確実に動く **Run ボタンクリック** に統一する。これで「キーボードショートカット動作」と「機能自体の動作」を分離できる
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

実装者を派遣して修正します。

**Test results: 7/8 pass, 1/8 fail**

What changed in `/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts`:

- **Test 5** (sidebar persistence): switched from `Meta+Enter` to `page.click("button:has-text('Run')")` — now passes
- **Test 6** (error panel): switched from `Meta+Enter` to `page.click("button:has-text('Run')")` — now passes
- **Test 8** (empty results): switched from `Meta+Enter` to `page.click("button:has-text('Run')")` — now passes
- **Test 3** (Cmd+Enter keyboard shortcut): switched from `page.keyboard.press("Meta+Enter")` to the explicit `down("Meta") / press("Enter") / up("Meta")` sequence — still fails

Also committed: `sql-editor/playwright.config.ts` (use deployed URL via `E2E_BASE_URL`) and `sql-editor/.gitignore` (ignore `test-results/`, `playwright-report/`, `playwright/.cache`).

**Concern — Test 3 (Cmd+Enter):** The explicit modifier down/up approach does not fix the underlying issue. Headless Chromium does not reliably dispatch the keyboard event in a way that CodeMirror's `Mod-Enter` keybinding catches. The event reaches the browser but CodeMirror's view plugin doesn't fire. Possible next steps to investigate:

1. Use `page.evaluate()` to dispatch a synthetic keyboard event directly on the CodeMirror editor element
2. Inject a JavaScript call to dispatch the CodeMirror command programmatically
3. Keep this one test as a known-flaky/skip and accept 7/8 coverage

  "description": "Fix Cmd+Enter test with evaluate",

  "prompt": "Fix the last failing Playwright test in SQL Editor: \"Cmd+Enter でクエリが実行される\".\n\n## Problem\n\nPlaywright's keyboard API cannot reliably trigger CodeMirror's `Mod-Enter` keybinding in headless chromium, even with explicit `keyboard.down(\"Meta\")` / `press(\"Enter\")` / `up(\"Meta\")`.\n\n## Solution\n\nUse `page.evaluate()` to dispatch a synthetic KeyboardEvent directly on the CodeMirror content element. This bypasses Playwright's keyboard layer and triggers CodeMirror's internal handler directly.\n\n## File\n\n- /Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts\n\n## Required Change\n\nReplace the Test 3 \"Cmd+Enter でクエリが実行される\" test body with:\n\n```typescript\ntest(\"Cmd+Enter でクエリが実行される\", async ({ page }) => {\n  const content = page.locator(\".cm-content\");\n  await content.click();\n  await page.keyboard.press(\"Meta+a\");\n  await page.keyboard.type(\"SELECT action FROM streaming.linear_events LIMIT 1\");\n\n  // Dispatch a synthetic Cmd+Enter KeyboardEvent directly to the CodeMirror content.\n  // Playwright's keyboard API doesn't reliably trigger CodeMirror's Mod-Enter binding\n  // in headless chromium, so we dispatch the event manually.\n  await content.evaluate((el) => {\n    el.dispatchEvent(\n      new KeyboardEvent(\"keydown\", {\n        key: \"Enter\",\n        code: \"Enter\",\n        keyCode: 13,\n        metaKey: true,\n        bubbles: true,\n        cancelable: true,\n      })\n    );\n  });\n\n  await expect(page.locator(\"table\")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });\n  await expect(page.locator(\"text=1 rows\")).toBeVisible();\n});\n```\n\n## Steps\n\n1. If Test 3 STILL fails, try a second approach: find the actual target element via CodeMirror's `.cm-editor` and dispatch the event there. 3	test.describe("SQL Editor", () => {
4	  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
5	    await page.goto("/");
6	    // CodeMirror がマウントされるのを待つ
7	    await page.waitForSelector(".cm-editor");
8	  });
9	
10	  test("エディターが表示され SQL を入力できる", async ({ page }) => {
11	    const editor = page.locator(".cm-editor");
12	    await expect(editor).toBeVisible();
13	    // CodeMirror のコンテンツ領域にテキストが存在する
14	    const content = page.locator(".cm-content");
15	    await expect(content).toContainText("R2 Data Catalog");
16	  });
17	
18	  test("Run ボタンクリックでクエリが実行される", async ({ page }) => {
19	    // デフォルトのプレースホルダーを消して SQL を入力
20	    const content = page.locator(".cm-content");
21	    await content.click();
22	    await page.keyboard.press("Meta+a");
23	    await page.keyboard.type("SELECT action, type FROM streaming.linear_events LIMIT 2");
24	
25	    // Run ボタンをクリック
26	    await page.click("button:has-text('Run')");
27	
28	    // 結果が表示されるのを待つ
29	    await expect(page.locator("table")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
30	    // 行数表示を確認
31	    await expect(page.locator("text=2 rows")).toBeVisible();

34	  test("Cmd+Enter でクエリが実行される", async ({ page }) => {
35	    const content = page.locator(".cm-content");
36	    await content.click();
37	    await page.keyboard.press("Meta+a");
38	    await page.keyboard.type("SELECT action FROM streaming.linear_events LIMIT 1");
39	
40	    // Dispatch a synthetic Cmd+Enter KeyboardEvent directly to the CodeMirror content.
41	    // Playwright's keyboard API doesn't reliably trigger CodeMirror's Mod-Enter binding
42	    // in headless chromium, so we dispatch the event manually.
43	    // Note: navigator.platform is "MacIntel" in headless Chromium on macOS, so
44	    // CodeMirror's browser.mac=true and Mod-Enter maps to metaKey (not ctrlKey).
45	    // We also use Prec.highest on the keymap in Editor.tsx to ensure our handler
46	    // takes priority over basicSetup's defaultKeymap (which also binds Mod-Enter).
47	    await content.evaluate((el) => {
48	      el.dispatchEvent(
49	        new KeyboardEvent("keydown", {
50	          key: "Enter",
51	          code: "Enter",
52	          keyCode: 13,
53	          metaKey: true,
54	          bubbles: true,
55	          cancelable: true,
56	        })
57	      );
58	    });
59	
60	    await expect(page.locator("table")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
61	    await expect(page.locator("text=1 rows")).toBeVisible();
62	  });
63	
64	  test("サイドバーにテーブル一覧が表示される", async ({ page }) => {
65	    // namespace ヘッダー
66	    await expect(page.locator("text=STREAMING").first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });
67	    // テーブル名
68	    await expect(page.locator("button:has-text('linear_events')")).toBeVisible();
69	  });
70	
71	  test("クエリ結果表示後もサイドバーが消えない", async ({ page }) => {

80	    await page.click("button:has-text('Run')");

82	    // 結果が表示されるのを待つ

> AGENT

発見しました! PR #62 のレビュー対応で追加した `quoteIdentifier` の `"` 引用符が R2 SQL では**名前の一部として解釈**されてしまい、namespace が見つかりません。Gemini のレビュー指摘は R2 SQL には適用できませんでした。

1	import { Hono } from "hono";
2	import type { Env, R2SqlResponse } from "../types";
3	import { R2_SQL_ACCOUNT_ID, R2_SQL_BUCKET_NAME } from "../types";
4	
5	const catalog = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();
6	
7	async function executeR2Sql(token: string, sql: string): Promise<R2SqlResponse> {
8	  const r2SqlUrl = `https://api.sql.cloudflarestorage.com/api/v1/accounts/${R2_SQL_ACCOUNT_ID}/r2-sql/query/${R2_SQL_BUCKET_NAME}`;
9	  const response = await fetch(r2SqlUrl, {
10	    method: "POST",
11	    headers: {
12	      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
13	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
14	    },
15	    body: JSON.stringify({ query: sql }),
16	  });
17	  if (!response.ok) {
18	    throw new Error(`R2 SQL error: ${response.status}`);
19	  }
20	  return (await response.json()) as R2SqlResponse;
21	}
22	
23	function quoteIdentifier(id: string): string {
24	  return `"${id.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
25	}
26	
27	catalog.get("/tables", async (c) => {
28	  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();
29	  if (!token) {
30	    return c.json({ error: "R2 SQL token not configured" }, 500);
31	  }
32	
33	  const namespaces = await executeR2Sql(token, "SHOW NAMESPACES");
34	
35	  const tables = (
36	    await Promise.all(
37	      (namespaces.result?.rows ?? []).map(async (ns) => {
38	        const nsName = Object.values(ns)[0] as string;
39	        const tblResult = await executeR2Sql(token, `SHOW TABLES IN ${quoteIdentifier(nsName)}`);
40	        return (tblResult.result?.rows ?? []).map((tbl) => ({
41	          namespace: nsName,
42	          table: Object.values(tbl)[0] as string,
43	        }));
44	      })
45	    )
46	  ).flat();
47	
48	  return c.json({ tables });
49	});
50	
51	catalog.get("/describe/:namespace/:table", async (c) => {
52	  const { namespace, table } = c.req.param();
53	  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();
54	  if (!token) {
55	    return c.json({ error: "R2 SQL token not configured" }, 500);
56	  }
57	
58	  const raw = await executeR2Sql(
59	    token,
60	    `DESCRIBE ${quoteIdentifier(namespace)}.${quoteIdentifier(table)}`
61	  );
62	  return c.json({ data: raw.result?.rows ?? [] });
63	});
64	
65	export default catalog;
66

index 35870dd..9d0fccc 100644
--- a/sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts
+++ b/sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts
@@ -37,10 +37,25 @@ test.describe("SQL Editor", () => {
     await page.keyboard.press("Meta+a");
     await page.keyboard.type("SELECT action FROM streaming.linear_events LIMIT 1");
 
-    // explicit modifier down/up is more reliable in headless chromium
-    await page.keyboard.down("Meta");
-    await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
-    await page.keyboard.up("Meta");
+    // Dispatch a synthetic Cmd+Enter KeyboardEvent directly to the CodeMirror content.
+    // Playwright's keyboard API doesn't reliably trigger CodeMirror's Mod-Enter binding
+    // in headless chromium, so we dispatch the event manually.
+    // Note: navigator.platform is "MacIntel" in headless Chromium on macOS, so
+    // CodeMirror's browser.mac=true and Mod-Enter maps to metaKey (not ctrlKey).
+    // We also use Prec.highest on the keymap in Editor.tsx to ensure our handler
+    // takes priority over basicSetup's defaultKeymap (which also binds Mod-Enter).
+    await content.evaluate((el) => {
+      el.dispatchEvent(
+        new KeyboardEvent("keydown", {
+          key: "Enter",
+          code: "Enter",
+          keyCode: 13,
+          metaKey: true,
+          bubbles: true,
+          cancelable: true,
+        })
+      );
+    });
 
     await expect(page.locator("table")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
     await expect(page.locator("text=1 rows")).toBeVisible();

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`

- CodeMirror の `Mod-Enter` を Playwright で発火させるには synthetic KeyboardEvent + `Prec.highest` 両方が必要だった（headless chromium での既知の制約）
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Typing `FROM ` shows table candidates, `SELECT ` shows column candidates.\n- **Playwright E2E tests (8 cases)**: editor render, Run button, Cmd+Enter, sidebar visibility, regression (sidebar persists after results), error display, table click insertion, empty results. All 8 pass against deployed environment.\n- **Bug fix**: PR #62 introduced double-quoted identifiers (`\"streaming\"`) in catalog queries based on a Gemini review suggestion, but R2 SQL doesn't support that syntax — replaced with strict `assertIdentifier` regex validation that rejects invalid identifiers with 400. Catalog API was returning 500 in production prior to this fix.\n\n## Changes\n- `src/hooks/useCatalog.ts` — Promise.all describeTable for all tables, builds `SqlSchema` (Record<string, string[]>)\n- `src/components/Editor.tsx` — Compartment + Prec.highest keymap for reliable Mod-Enter\n- `src/lib/api.ts` — concrete `ColumnInfo` type (replaces `Record<string, unknown>[]`)\n- `src/App.tsx` — passes `schema` prop to Editor\n- `worker/api/catalog.ts` — `assertIdentifier` strict validation\n- `e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts` — 8 Playwright tests\n- `playwright.config.ts` — points to deployed env via `E2E_BASE_URL`\n- `biome.json` — exclude playwright artifacts\n- `.gitignore` — exclude test-results, playwright-report, playwright/.cache\n\n## Test plan\n- [x] `pnpm exec biome check .` — pass\n- [x] `pnpm exec tsc -b` — pass\n- [x] `pnpm exec playwright test` — **8/8 pass**\n- [x] Production catalog API returns table list and column schema correctly\n- [x] Production deployment current\n- [ ] Manual: open https://sql-editor.ta93abe.workers.dev, type `SELECT ` and verify column autocomplete suggestions appear\n\nRefs TA-453 (Phase 1.5)\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1",

1	import { sql } from "@codemirror/lang-sql";
2	import { Compartment, EditorState, Prec } from "@codemirror/state";
3	import { EditorView, keymap } from "@codemirror/view";
4	import { basicSetup } from "codemirror";
5	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
6	import type { SqlSchema } from "../hooks/useCatalog";
7	
8	interface EditorProps {
9	  value: string;
10	  onChange: (value: string) => void;
11	  onRun: () => void;
12	  schema?: SqlSchema;
13	}
14	
15	export default function Editor({ value, onChange, onRun, schema }: EditorProps) {
16	  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
17	  const viewRef = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);
18	  const sqlCompartment = useRef(new Compartment());
19	  const onChangeRef = useRef(onChange);
20	  const onRunRef = useRef(onRun);
21	
22	  onChangeRef.current = onChange;
23	  onRunRef.current = onRun;
24	
25	  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: CodeMirror is imperative; value is only used for initial doc, subsequent syncs happen via the second useEffect
26	  useEffect(() => {
27	    if (!containerRef.current) return;
28	
29	    const view = new EditorView({
30	      state: EditorState.create({
31	        doc: value,
32	        extensions: [
33	          basicSetup,
34	          sqlCompartment.current.of(sql()),
35	          Prec.highest(
36	            keymap.of([
37	              {
38	                key: "Mod-Enter",
39	                run: () => {
40	                  onRunRef.current();
41	                  return true;
42	                },
43	              },
44	            ])
45	          ),
46	          EditorView.updateListener.of((update) => {
47	            if (update.docChanged) {
48	              onChangeRef.current(update.state.doc.toString());
49	            }
50	          }),
51	          EditorView.theme({
52	            "&": { height: "100%" },
53	            ".cm-scroller": { overflow: "auto" },
54	            ".cm-content": { padding: "12px 0" },
55	          }),
56	        ],
57	      }),
58	      parent: containerRef.current,
59	    });
60	
61	    viewRef.current = view;
62	    return () => view.destroy();
63	  }, []);
64	
65	  // Sync external value changes (e.g., sidebar insert)
66	  useEffect(() => {
67	    const view = viewRef.current;
68	    if (view && view.state.doc.toString() !== value) {
69	      view.dispatch({
70	        changes: { from: 0, to: view.state.doc.length, insert: value },
71	      });
72	    }
73	  }, [value]);
74	
75	  // Update SQL schema when catalog data arrives
76	  useEffect(() => {
77	    const view = viewRef.current;
78	    if (view && schema && Object.keys(schema).length > 0) {
79	      view.dispatch({
80	        effects: sqlCompartment.current.reconfigure(sql({ schema })),
81	      });
82	    }
83	  }, [schema]);
84	
85	  return <div ref={containerRef} style={{ height: "100%", overflow: "auto" }} />;
86	}
87	
```

> AGENT

デプロイ + プッシュ完了。

**修正内容:**
1. **Tab で補完確定**: `@codemirror/autocomplete` の `acceptCompletion` を Tab キーにバインド（補完ポップアップが開いているときのみ）
2. **サイドバー消失防止**:
   - 右側カラム div に `minWidth: 0`（flex item のデフォルト `min-width: auto` が原因で広いテーブルがレイアウトを押し広げていた）
   - Sidebar に `flexShrink: 0`（240px を保証）

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **flex の落とし穴**: flex item は `min-width: auto` がデフォルトで、子要素のコンテンツ幅より縮みません。`minWidth: 0` を明示すると本来の `flex: 1` 配分が機能する。これは flexbox の最も有名な gotcha
- **E2E テストの盲点**: Playwright の `toBeVisible()` は DOM に存在し `display: none` でないことを確認するだけで、実際にビューポート内にあるかは見ない。`boundingBox()` で位置検証する必要がある
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

ブラウザで https://sql-editor.ta93abe.workers.dev を再読み込みして確認してください。

1	import type { TableInfo } from "../hooks/useCatalog";
2	
3	interface SidebarProps {
4	  tables: TableInfo[];
5	  loading: boolean;
6	  onInsert: (text: string) => void;
7	}
8	
9	export default function Sidebar({ tables, loading, onInsert }: SidebarProps) {
10	  const grouped = tables.reduce<Record<string, TableInfo[]>>((acc, t) => {
11	    if (!acc[t.namespace]) {
12	      acc[t.namespace] = [];
13	    }
14	    acc[t.namespace].push(t);
15	    return acc;
16	  }, {});
17	
18	  return (
19	    <div
20	      style={{
21	        width: 240,
22	        flexShrink: 0,
23	        borderRight: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
24	        overflow: "auto",
25	        padding: "12px 0",
26	        fontSize: 13,
27	      }}
28	    >
29	      <div
30	        style={{
31	          padding: "0 12px 8px",
32	          fontWeight: 600,
33	          color: "#374151",
34	        }}
35	      >
36	        Tables
37	      </div>
38	      {loading ? (
39	        <div style={{ padding: "0 12px", color: "#888" }}>Loading...</div>
40	      ) : (
41	        Object.entries(grouped).map(([ns, tbls]) => (
42	          <div key={ns} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
43	            <div
44	              style={{
45	                padding: "4px 12px",
46	                fontWeight: 600,
47	                color: "#6b7280",
48	                fontSize: 11,
49	                textTransform: "uppercase",
50	                letterSpacing: "0.05em",
51	              }}
52	            >
53	              {ns}
54	            </div>
55	            {tbls.map((t) => (
56	              <button
57	                type="button"
58	                key={`${t.namespace}.${t.table}`}
59	                onClick={() => onInsert(`${t.namespace}.${t.table}`)}
60	                style={{
61	                  display: "block",
62	                  width: "100%",
63	                  textAlign: "left",
64	                  padding: "4px 12px 4px 20px",
65	                  background: "none",
66	                  border: "none",
67	                  cursor: "pointer",
68	                  color: "#1f2937",
69	                  fontSize: 13,
70	                }}
71	                title={`Click to insert ${t.namespace}.${t.table}`}
72	              >
73	                {t.table}
74	              </button>
75	            ))}
76	          </div>
77	        ))
78	      )}
79	    </div>
80	  );
81	}
82

1	import type { QueryResult } from "../lib/api";
2	
3	interface ToolbarProps {
4	  loading: boolean;
5	  onRun: () => void;
6	  result: QueryResult | null;
7	}
8	
9	export default function Toolbar({ loading, onRun, result }: ToolbarProps) {
10	  return (
11	    <div
12	      style={{
13	        padding: "6px 16px",
14	        borderTop: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
15	        borderBottom: "1px solid #e5e7eb",
16	        display: "flex",
17	        alignItems: "center",
18	        gap: 12,
19	      }}
20	    >
21	      <button
22	        type="button"
23	        onClick={onRun}
24	        disabled={loading}
25	        style={{
26	          padding: "4px 16px",
27	          background: loading ? "#9ca3af" : "#2563eb",
28	          color: "white",
29	          border: "none",
30	          borderRadius: 4,
31	          cursor: loading ? "not-allowed" : "pointer",
32	          fontWeight: 600,
33	          fontSize: 13,
34	        }}
35	      >
36	        {loading ? "Running..." : "Run (R2 SQL)"}
37	      </button>
38	      <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#888" }}>Cmd+Enter</span>
39	      {result?.elapsed && (
40	        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "#888", marginLeft: "auto" }}>{result.elapsed}ms</span>
41	      )}
42	    </div>
43	  );
44	}
45

  "content": "import type { QueryResult } from \"../lib/api\";\n\ninterface ResultPanelProps {\n  result: QueryResult | null;\n  loading: boolean;\n}\n\nconst MUTED_COLOR = \"light-dark(#888, #a1a1aa)\";\nconst SUCCESS_COLOR = \"light-dark(#16a34a, #22c55e)\";\nconst BORDER_COLOR = \"light-dark(#e5e7eb, #27272a)\";\nconst ROW_BORDER = \"light-dark(#f3f4f6, #1f1f23)\";\nconst HEADER_BG = \"light-dark(#ffffff, #0f0f10)\";\nconst TEXT_HEADER = \"light-dark(#374151, #d4d4d8)\";\nconst TEXT_MUTED = \"light-dark(#9ca3af, #71717a)\";\n\nexport default function ResultPanel({ result, loading }: ResultPanelProps) {\n  if (loading) {\n    return <div style={{ padding: 16, color: MUTED_COLOR }}>クエリを実行中...</div>;\n  }\n\n  if (!result) {\n    return (\n      <div style={{ padding: 16, color: MUTED_COLOR }}>\n        Cmd+Enter または Run ボタンでクエリを実行\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (result.error) {\n    return (\n      <div style={{ padding: 16 }}>\n        <div\n          style={{\n            background: \"light-dark(#fef2f2, #450a0a)\",\n            border: \"1px solid light-dark(#fecaca, #7f1d1d)\",\n            borderRadius: 6,\n            padding: 12,\n          }}\n        >\n          <div\n            style={{\n              fontWeight: 600,\n              color: \"light-dark(#dc2626, #f87171)\",\n              marginBottom: 4,\n            }}\n          >\n            Query Error {result.engine ? `· ${result.engine}` : \"\"}\n          </div>\n          <pre\n            style={{\n              margin: 0,\n              whiteSpace: \"pre-wrap\",\n              fontSize: 13,\n              color: \"light-dark(#991b1b, #fca5a5)\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {result.error}\n          </pre>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const rows = result.data ?? [];\n  if (rows.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div style={{ padding: 16 }}>\n        <div style={{ color: SUCCESS_COLOR, fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 8 }}>\n          0 rows · {result.elapsed}ms · {result.engine}\n        </div>\n        <div style={{ color: MUTED_COLOR }}>クエリは成功しましたが、結果は0件です。</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const columns = Object.keys(rows[0]);\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ padding: \"8px 16px\" }}>\n      <div\n        style={{\n          marginBottom: 8,\n          color: SUCCESS_COLOR,\n          fontWeight: 600,\n          fontSize: 13,\n        }}\n      >\n        {rows.length} rows · {result.elapsed}ms · {result.engine}\n      </div>\n      <div style={{ overflow: \"auto\" }}>\n        <table\n          style={{\n            width: \"100%\",\n            borderCollapse: \"collapse\",\n            fontSize: 13,\n          }}\n        >\n          <thead>\n            <tr>\n              <th\n                style={{\n                  padding: \"6px 12px\",\n                  borderBottom: `2px solid ${BORDER_COLOR}`,\n                  textAlign: \"left\",\n                  fontWeight: 600,\n                  color: TEXT_HEADER,\n                  position: \"sticky\",\n                  top: 0,\n                  background: HEADER_BG,\n                }}\n              >\n                #\n              </th>\n              {columns.map((col) => (\n                <th\n                  key={col}\n                  style={{\n                    padding: \"6px 12px\",\n                    borderBottom: `2px solid ${BORDER_COLOR}`,\n                    textAlign: \"left\",\n                    fontWeight: 600,\n                    color: TEXT_HEADER,\n                    position: \"sticky\",\n                    top: 0,\n                    background: HEADER_BG,\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {col}\n                </th>\n              ))}\n            </tr>\n          </thead>\n          <tbody>\n            {rows.map((row, i) => (\n              // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: SQL result rows have no stable unique key\n              <tr key={i} style={{ borderBottom: `1px solid ${ROW_BORDER}` }}>\n                <td\n                  style={{\n                    padding: \"6px 12px\",\n                    color: TEXT_MUTED,\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {i + 1}\n                </td>\n                {columns.map((col) => (\n                  <td\n                    key={col}\n                    style={{\n                      padding: \"6px 12px\",\n                      maxWidth: 300,\n                      overflow: \"hidden\",\n                      textOverflow: \"ellipsis\",\n                      whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {renderCell(row[col])}\n                  </td>\n                ))}\n              </tr>\n            ))}\n          </tbody>\n        </table>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction renderCell(value: unknown): string {\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n    return \"NULL\";\n  }\n  if (typeof value === \"object\") {\n    return JSON.stringify(value);\n  }\n  return String(value);\n}\n"

> AGENT

ダークモード対応完了・デプロイ・プッシュ済み。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **`light-dark()` CSS 関数**: CSS Colors Module Level 5 の新機能で、インラインスタイルから直接 `light-dark(light値, dark値)` で両モードの色を指定できる。`color-scheme` プロパティと組み合わせると、Media Query なしで切り替えが動く
- **CodeMirror の Compartment パターン**: Theme も `Compartment` で包むことで、`matchMedia('change')` イベントに応じて `reconfigure` → エディター再生成せずリアルタイム切り替えが可能
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

OS のダークモードを切り替えて https://sql-editor.ta93abe.workers.dev を確認してみてください。

> DEVELOPER

Rust WASMで実装するとパフォーマンス上がったりするの？

> AGENT

ケースバイケースです。この SQL Editor の場合、**現状のボトルネックは WASM で解決できません**。

## 現状のボトルネック

| 処理 | 支配的な制約 | Rust WASM で改善? |
|---|---|---|
| クエリ実行 | ネットワーク + R2 SQL サーバー | **No** (サーバー側で処理) |
| エディター操作 | CodeMirror (すでに最適化済) | **No** |
| 結果テーブル描画 | DOM 操作 (React/ブラウザ) | **No** (DOM は JS 経由必須) |
| API レスポンスパース | JSON.parse (ネイティブ実装) | **No** (すでに C++ ネイティブ) |

## Rust WASM が効くケース

- **大量データの変換**: 100 万行以上のフィルタ・集計・ソート
- **カスタム SQL パーサ/フォーマッタ**: 独自構文解析など
- **データ可視化の計算**: ヒストグラム、クラスタリングなど
- **圧縮・暗号化**: ネイティブ処理が重い場合

## JS↔WASM 境界コスト

WASM と JS 間のデータ受け渡しにはシリアライズ/デシリアライズのコストがあります。**小さな処理を頻繁に呼ぶと JS より遅くなる**ケースも多いです。1 回の呼び出しで大量データを処理する設計が必須です。

## この Editor での現実解

Phase 2 の **DuckDB WASM 統合**がまさに WASM の正しい使い方です(C++→WASM だが同じ原理):
- ブラウザ内で Iceberg に直接クエリ(サーバー往復なし)
- 数百万行の集計がローカルで完結
- Rust WASM を別途書くよりも、既存の DuckDB を使う方が遥かに効果的

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **「WASM = 速い」は誤解**: DOM 操作や小さな処理では JS の方が速いことも多い
- WASM が真価を発揮するのは「純粋計算で大きな塊を処理する」場面 (画像処理、物理シミュ、DB エンジンなど)
- Cloudflare Workers にも Rust ランタイムがあるが、用途は「起動速度」「メモリ効率」「予測可能な性能」で、TypeScript の代替というより使い分けの選択肢
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Phase 2 の DuckDB WASM 統合を進めるのが、パフォーマンス観点でも費用対効果が最も高いです。

gemini-code-assist[bot] @ sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts:27
![high](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/high-priority.svg)

Using `Promise.all` here makes the catalog fetch fragile. If any single `describeTable` call fails (e.g., due to a network error or a table being deleted), the entire process rejects and the sidebar will appear empty. Additionally, fetching all table schemas in parallel without a concurrency limit could lead to performance issues or rate limiting if the catalog grows large. Consider wrapping individual calls in a try-catch block
---
gemini-code-assist[bot] @ sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts:10
![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)

To enable better autocomplete for schema-qualified identifiers (e.g., `namespace.table`), the `SqlSchema` should be structured as a nested object where the first level represents the namespace and the second level represents the table name. This allows the SQL extension to correctly suggest tables after a namespace prefix is typed.

```suggestion
export type SqlSchema = Record<string, Record<string, string[]>>;
```
---

Copilot @ sql-editor/worker/api/catalog.ts:45
`assertIdentifier(nsName)` can throw, but `/tables` doesn’t catch it. If a namespace name ever fails the pattern (even due to upstream changes), this handler will error out with an unhandled exception instead of returning a consistent JSON error response. Consider wrapping the namespace/table discovery loop in try/catch and returning a structured error (or skipping invalid namespaces) to avoid surprising 500s.
---
Copilot @ sql-editor/worker/api/catalog.ts:72
The invalid-identifier path is detected by checking `err.message.startsWith("Invalid identifier")`, which is brittle and couples behavior to the exact error string. Prefer a dedicated error class (e.g., `InvalidIdentifierError`) or a tagged error shape so you can reliably map it to HTTP 400 without string parsing.
---
Copilot @ sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts:32
Using `Promise.all(list.map(describeTable...))` creates an unbounded fan-out of requests and will fail the entire catalog load if any single describe call rejects (network error, transient 5xx, etc.), resulting in no tables/schema even when most describes succeeded. Consider using `Promise.allSettled` (or a concurrency limit) and building schema/tables from successful results while handling individual failures gracefully.

Copilot @ sql-editor/e2e/sql-editor.spec.ts:23
These tests use `Meta+a` to select all text, which is macOS-specific and will fail on Linux/Windows runners (and potentially for non-mac developer machines). Consider using `ControlOrMeta+A` (Playwright supports `ControlOrMeta`) or branching on `process.platform` so the suite is portable.

Copilot @ sql-editor/playwright.config.ts:4
`baseURL` defaults to the deployed production URL. This makes `pnpm run test:e2e` easy to run accidentally against prod and increases flakiness due to network/data variability. Consider requiring `E2E_BASE_URL` (no default) or configuring Playwright `webServer` to run locally and default `baseURL` to that.
```suggestion
const BASE_URL = process.env.E2E_BASE_URL;

if (!BASE_URL) {
  throw new Error("E2E_BASE_URL must be set for Playwright E2E tests.");
}
```

_⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_

**Make schema prefetch tolerant of partial failures.**

This fires one `describeTable` request per table and only calls `setTables` after the slowest one finishes. A single rejected request sends the whole hook through Line 38 and clears the catalog, while a JSON `{ error }` response is silently downgraded to `columns: []` on Line 25. That makes autocomplete brittle and hard to debug; if this catalog grows, the full fan-out will also get expensive.

<details>
<s
```

主要なレビュー指摘を整理します:

| 優先度 | 指摘 | 出典 |
|---|---|---|
| High | `Promise.all` → `allSettled` で部分失敗耐性 | Gemini/Copilot/CodeRabbit |
| High | `assertIdentifier` が /tables で throw されるとハンドリングされない | Copilot |
| Medium | `Invalid identifier` を string match しているのは脆い → error class | Copilot |
| Medium | `SqlSchema` をネスト構造 `Record<string, Record<string, string[]>>` に | Gemini |
| Medium | `Meta+a` → `ControlOrMeta+A` でクロスプラットフォーム | Copilot/CodeRabbit |
| Medium | `playwright.config.ts` が本番 URL をデフォルト（意図せず本番にテスト） | Copilot/CodeRabbit |

スキップするもの: 色トークンの共通化（ダークモードを revert したので大半が不要）、/describe レスポンスの型正規化（現状動作に影響なし）。

1	import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
2	import { type ColumnInfo, describeTable, fetchTables, type TablesResult } from "../lib/api";
3	
4	export interface TableInfo {
5	  namespace: string;
6	  table: string;
7	  columns?: ColumnInfo[];
8	}
9	
10	export type SqlSchema = Record<string, string[]>;
11	
12	export function useCatalog() {
13	  const [tables, setTables] = useState<TableInfo[]>([]);
14	  const [schema, setSchema] = useState<SqlSchema>({});
15	  const [loadingTables, setLoadingTables] = useState(true);
16	
17	  useEffect(() => {
18	    fetchTables()
19	      .then(async (res: TablesResult) => {
20	        const list = res.tables ?? [];
21	
22	        const described = await Promise.all(
23	          list.map(async (t) => {
24	            const desc = await describeTable(t.namespace, t.table);
25	            return { namespace: t.namespace, table: t.table, columns: desc.data ?? [] };
26	          })
27	        );
28	
29	        const newSchema: SqlSchema = {};
30	        const enriched: TableInfo[] = [];
31	        for (const d of described) {
32	          newSchema[`${d.namespace}.${d.table}`] = d.columns.map((c) => c.column_name);
33	          enriched.push({ namespace: d.namespace, table: d.table, columns: d.columns });
34	        }
35	        setTables(enriched);
36	        setSchema(newSchema);
37	      })
38	      .catch(() => setTables([]))
39	      .finally(() => setLoadingTables(false));
40	  }, []);
41	
42	  return { tables, schema, loadingTables };
43	}
44	
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- `@codemirror/lang-sql` 6.10 の `SQLNamespace` 型はネスト構造をサポート: `{ namespace: { table: [columns] } }` だと `namespace.` で tables を補完、`namespace.table.` で columns を補完という多段の補完ツリーが効く
- `Promise.allSettled` を使うと各プロミスが `{ status: "fulfilled" | "rejected", value/reason }` を返し、個別失敗を吸収できる。`console.warn` で失敗テーブルを記録すると後で原因追跡可能
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/sql-editor/src/hooks/useCatalog.ts",
  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { type ColumnInfo, describeTable, fetchTables, type TablesResult } from \"../lib/api\";\n\nexport interface TableInfo {\n  namespace: string;\n  table: string;\n  columns?: ColumnInfo[];\n}\n\n// Nested schema format understood by @codemirror/lang-sql:\n//   { namespace: { table: [column1, column2, ...] } }\n// This enables multi-part identifier autocompletion (e.g. typing `streaming.`\n// suggests table names, typing `streaming.linear_events.` suggests columns).\nexport type SqlSchema = Record<string, Record<string, string[]>>;\n\nexport function useCatalog() {\n  const [tables, setTables] = useState<TableInfo[]>([]);\n  const [schema, setSchema] = useState<SqlSchema>({});\n  const [loadingTables, setLoadingTables] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchTables()\n      .then(async (res: TablesResult) => {\n        const list = res.tables ?? [];\n\n        // Use allSettled so one failing DESCRIBE doesn't wipe the whole catalog.\n        const settled = await Promise.allSettled(\n          list.map(async (t) => {\n            const desc = await describeTable(t.namespace, t.table);\n            return { namespace: t.namespace, table: t.table, columns: desc.data ?? [] };\n          })\n        );\n\n        const newSchema: SqlSchema = {};\n        const enriched: TableInfo[] = [];\n        for (let i = 0; i < settled.length; i++) {\n          const outcome = settled[i];\n          if (outcome.status === \"fulfilled\") {\n            const d = outcome.value;\n            if (!newSchema[d.namespace]) newSchema[d.namespace] = {};\n            newSchema[d.namespace][d.table] = d.columns.map((c) => c.column_name);\n            enriched.push({ namespace: d.namespace, table: d.table, columns: d.columns });\n          } else {\n            const t = list[i];\n            console.warn(`Failed to describe ${t.namespace}.${t.table}:`, outcome.reason);\n            // Still include the table (without columns) so it shows up in the sidebar.\n            enriched.push({ namespace: t.namespace, table: t.table });\n          }\n        }\n        setTables(enriched);\n        setSchema(newSchema);\n      })\n      .catch(() => setTables([]))\n      .finally(() => setLoadingTables(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  return { tables, schema, loadingTables };\n}\n"
}

1	import { Hono } from "hono";
2	import type { Env, R2SqlResponse } from "../types";
3	import { R2_SQL_ACCOUNT_ID, R2_SQL_BUCKET_NAME } from "../types";
4	
5	const catalog = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();
6	
7	async function executeR2Sql(token: string, sql: string): Promise<R2SqlResponse> {
8	  const r2SqlUrl = `https://api.sql.cloudflarestorage.com/api/v1/accounts/${R2_SQL_ACCOUNT_ID}/r2-sql/query/${R2_SQL_BUCKET_NAME}`;
9	  const response = await fetch(r2SqlUrl, {
10	    method: "POST",
11	    headers: {
12	      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
13	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
14	    },
15	    body: JSON.stringify({ query: sql }),
16	  });
17	  if (!response.ok) {
18	    throw new Error(`R2 SQL error: ${response.status}`);
19	  }
20	  return (await response.json()) as R2SqlResponse;
21	}
22	
23	const IDENTIFIER_PATTERN = /^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/;
24	
25	function assertIdentifier(id: string): string {
26	  if (!IDENTIFIER_PATTERN.test(id)) {
27	    throw new Error(`Invalid identifier: ${id}`);
28	  }
29	  return id;
30	}
31	
32	catalog.get("/tables", async (c) => {
33	  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();
34	  if (!token) {
35	    return c.json({ error: "R2 SQL token not configured" }, 500);
36	  }
37	
38	  const namespaces = await executeR2Sql(token, "SHOW NAMESPACES");
39	
40	  const tables = (
41	    await Promise.all(
42	      (namespaces.result?.rows ?? []).map(async (ns) => {
43	        const nsName = Object.values(ns)[0] as string;
44	        const tblResult = await executeR2Sql(token, `SHOW TABLES IN ${assertIdentifier(nsName)}`);
45	        return (tblResult.result?.rows ?? []).map((tbl) => ({
46	          namespace: nsName,
47	          table: Object.values(tbl)[0] as string,
48	        }));
49	      })
50	    )
51	  ).flat();
52	
53	  return c.json({ tables });
54	});
55	
56	catalog.get("/describe/:namespace/:table", async (c) => {
57	  const { namespace, table } = c.req.param();
58	  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();
59	  if (!token) {
60	    return c.json({ error: "R2 SQL token not configured" }, 500);
61	  }
62	
63	  try {
64	    const raw = await executeR2Sql(
65	      token,
66	      `DESCRIBE ${assertIdentifier(namespace)}.${assertIdentifier(table)}`
67	    );
68	    return c.json({ data: raw.result?.rows ?? [] });
69	  } catch (err) {
70	    if (err instanceof Error && err.message.startsWith("Invalid identifier")) {
71	      return c.json({ error: err.message }, 400);
72	    }
73	    throw err;
74	  }
75	});
76	
77	export default catalog;
78

[]).map(async (ns) => {\n          const nsName = Object.values(ns)[0] as string;\n          const tblResult = await executeR2Sql(\n            token,\n            `SHOW TABLES IN ${assertIdentifier(nsName)}`\n          );\n          return (tblResult.result?.rows ?? []).map((tbl) => ({\n            namespace: nsName,\n            table: Object.values(tbl)[0] as string,\n          }));\n        })\n      )\n    ).flat();\n\n    return c.json({ tables });\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (err instanceof InvalidIdentifierError) {\n      return c.json({ error: err.message }, 400);\n    }\n    throw err;\n  }\n});\n\ncatalog.get(\"/describe/:namespace/:table\", async (c) => {\n  const { namespace, table } = c.req.param();\n  const token = await c.env.R2_SQL_TOKEN.get();\n  if (!token) {\n    return c.json({ error: \"R2 SQL token not configured\" }, 500);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const raw = await executeR2Sql(\n      token,\n      `DESCRIBE ${assertIdentifier(namespace)}.${assertIdentifier(table)}`\n    );\n    return c.json({ data: raw.result?.rows ?? [] });\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (err instanceof InvalidIdentifierError) {\n      return c.json({ error: err.message }, 400);\n    }\n    throw err;\n  }\n});\n\nexport default catalog;\n"
}

40	    // Dispatch a synthetic Cmd+Enter KeyboardEvent directly to the CodeMirror content.
41	    // Playwright's keyboard API doesn't reliably trigger CodeMirror's Mod-Enter binding
42	    // in headless chromium, so we dispatch the event manually.
43	    // Note: navigator.platform is "MacIntel" in headless Chromium on macOS, so
44	    // CodeMirror's browser.mac=true and Mod-Enter maps to metaKey (not ctrlKey).
45	    // We also use Prec.highest on the keymap in Editor.tsx to ensure our handler
46	    // takes priority over basicSetup's defaultKeymap (which also binds Mod-Enter).
47	    await content.evaluate((el) => {
48	      el.dispatchEvent(
49	        new KeyboardEvent("keydown", {
50	          key: "Enter",
51	          code: "Enter",
52	          keyCode: 13,
53	          metaKey: true,
54	          bubbles: true,
55	          cancelable: true,
56	        })
57	      );
58	    });
59	
60	    await expect(page.locator("table")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
61	    await expect(page.locator("text=1 rows")).toBeVisible();
62	  });
